Your notification came at 5:50 PM, 40 minutes before the original 6:30 PM time, so the delivery time became 7:00 PM. An order counts as late only if it arrives more than 15 minutes after the delivery time. Yours arrived 12 minutes after 7:00 PM, which is inside that window. I know it still felt slow, but under our rules it wasn't late. **#6504 – the time limit** We can only compensate a late delivery for up to 7 days after it's delivered. #6504 was delivered on October 16 at 8:00 PM, so the deadline was October 23 at 8:00 PM, yesterday evening. I'm sorry, but I can't make an exception to that. **#6502 – missing summer rolls: $9.00 refunded to your card** You reported this within our 48-hour window, so the summer rolls are covered.
